#undef main
// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L"'^T where L is lower triangular

bool referenceCholeskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu'"\\n\", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void referenceGeneratePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B"'^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B''^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool referenceValidateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L''^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L''^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e'"\\n\", maxError);
    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}


int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
    int rank, ranks;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);
    int failures = 0;
    for (size_t n : {size_t(0), size_t(1), size_t(7), size_t(8), size_t(9),
                     size_t(31), size_t(63), size_t(64), size_t(65),
                     size_t(127), size_t(129), size_t(257), size_t(513)}) {
        ProcessGrid grid(n);
        Matrix matrix(grid);
        generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(matrix, n);
        std::vector<double> input = gatherFactor(matrix, n);
        std::vector<double> expected;
        double inputError = 0.0, factorError = 0.0;
        if (rank == 0) {
            expected.resize(n * n);
            referenceGeneratePositiveDefiniteMatrix(expected, n);
            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i)
                for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j)
                    inputError = std::max(inputError, std::fabs(input[i*n+j] - expected[i*n+j]));
            if ("'!referenceCholeskyDecomposition(expected, n)) ++failures;
        }
        if (!choleskyDecomposition(matrix, n)) ++failures;
        std::vector<double> actual = gatherFactor(matrix, n);
        if (rank == 0) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
                factorError = std::max(factorError, std::fabs(actual[i] - expected[i]));
                if (!std::isfinite(actual[i])) ++failures;
            }
            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i)
                for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j)
                    if (actual[i*n+j] != 0.0) ++failures;
            if (inputError > 1e-10 || factorError > 1e-11) ++failures;
            printf("ranks=%d n=%zu input_error=%.3e factor_error=%.3e'"\\n\",
                   ranks, n, inputError, factorError);
        }
    }
    {
        const size_t n = 65;
        ProcessGrid grid(n);
        Matrix invalid(grid);
        for (size_t k = 0; k < grid.tiles; ++k) {
            if (k % grid.rows "'!= size_t(grid.row) || k % grid.cols != size_t(grid.col)) continue;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(grid.block, n-k*grid.block); ++i)
                invalid.tile(k,k)[i*grid.block+i] = k*grid.block+i == n-1 ? -1.0 : 1.0;
        }
        if (choleskyDecomposition(invalid, n)) ++failures;
    }
    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &failures, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    if (rank == 0) printf("Full-matrix checks: %s'"\\n\", failures ? \"FAILED\" : \"PASSED\");
    MPI_Finalize();
    return failures ? 1 : 0;
}

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+
+// All matrices use square, row-major tiles on a 2D block-cyclic process grid.
+// A rank stores O(n^2 / ranks) matrix entries and O(n * block / sqrt(ranks))
+// panel entries on a balanced grid; complete matrices are never replicated.
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int rank = 0, size = 0;
+    int rows = 0, cols = 0, row = 0, col = 0;
+    size_t block = 64, tiles = 0, localRows = 0, localCols = 0;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL, colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+
+    explicit ProcessGrid(size_t n) {
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        rows = dims[0];
+        cols = dims[1];
+        row = rank / cols;
+        col = rank % cols;
+        // Smaller tiles expose enough work for small matrices and large grids.
+        while (block > 8 && n / block < 2 * static_cast<size_t>(std::max(rows, cols)))
+            block /= 2;
+        tiles = n / block + (n % block != 0);
+        localRows = count(tiles, row, rows);
+        localCols = count(tiles, col, cols);
+        // Allgatherv uses int counts/displacements for panel buffers.
+        const size_t maxPanelTiles = std::max(count(tiles, 0, rows),
+                                             count(tiles, 0, cols));
+        if (maxPanelTiles > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) / (block * block))
+            throw std::length_error("Matrix panels exceed MPI count limits");
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, col, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, col, row, &colComm);
+    }
+
+    static size_t count(size_t total, int coordinate, int stride) {
+        const size_t start = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+        return total > start ? 1 + (total - 1 - start) / static_cast<size_t>(stride) : 0;
+    }
+
+    size_t firstLocalRow(size_t first) const {
+        return first > static_cast<size_t>(row)
+            ? std::min(localRows, (first - row + rows - 1) / rows) : 0;
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        if (rowComm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        if (colComm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+    }
+};
+
+struct Matrix {
+    const ProcessGrid& grid;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    explicit Matrix(const ProcessGrid& g) : grid(g) {
+        const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+        if (g.localRows && g.localCols > data.max_size() / area / g.localRows)
+            throw std::length_error("Matrix is too large");
+        data.resize(g.localRows * g.localCols * area, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    double* local(size_t i, size_t j) {
+        return data.data() + (i * grid.localCols + j) * grid.block * grid.block;
+    }
+
+    const double* local(size_t i, size_t j) const {
+        return data.data() + (i * grid.localCols + j) * grid.block * grid.block;
+    }
+
+    double* tile(size_t i, size_t j) {
+        return local(i / grid.rows, j / grid.cols);
+    }
+};
+
+#if defined(__AVX2__)
+inline __m256d accumulateProduct(__m256d a, __m256d b, __m256d sum) {
+#if defined(__FMA__)
+    return _mm256_fmadd_pd(a, b, sum);
+#else
+    return _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_mul_pd(a, b), sum);
+#endif
+}
+
+template<bool Subtract>
+inline void storeSum(double* target, __m256d sum) {
+    const __m256d previous = _mm256_loadu_pd(target);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(target, Subtract ? _mm256_sub_pd(previous, sum)
+                                    : _mm256_add_pd(previous, sum));
+}
+#endif
+
+// Transposed RHS panels are reused for every local tile. The 4 x 8
+// register block avoids horizontal reductions and keeps independent SIMD
+// accumulators; other architectures use the same portable kernel below.
+template<bool Subtract>
+void multiplyTile(double* target, const double* left,
+                  const double* rightTransposed, size_t b) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < b; i += 4) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < b; j += 8) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__)
+            __m256d s00 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d s10 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d s20 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d s30 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < b; ++k) {
+                const double* rhs = rightTransposed + k * b + j;
+                const __m256d r0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(rhs);
+                const __m256d r1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(rhs + 4);
+                const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + i * b + k);
+                const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + (i + 1) * b + k);
+                const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + (i + 2) * b + k);
+                const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + (i + 3) * b + k);
+                s00 = accumulateProduct(a0, r0, s00);
+                s01 = accumulateProduct(a0, r1, s01);
+                s10 = accumulateProduct(a1, r0, s10);
+                s11 = accumulateProduct(a1, r1, s11);
+                s20 = accumulateProduct(a2, r0, s20);
+                s21 = accumulateProduct(a2, r1, s21);
+                s30 = accumulateProduct(a3, r0, s30);
+                s31 = accumulateProduct(a3, r1, s31);
+            }
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + i * b + j, s00);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + i * b + j + 4, s01);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 1) * b + j, s10);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 1) * b + j + 4, s11);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 2) * b + j, s20);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 2) * b + j + 4, s21);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 3) * b + j, s30);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 3) * b + j + 4, s31);
+#else
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < b; ++k) {
+                const double a0 = left[i * b + k];
+                const double a1 = left[(i + 1) * b + k];
+                const double a2 = left[(i + 2) * b + k];
+                const double a3 = left[(i + 3) * b + k];
+                const double* rhs = rightTransposed + k * b + j;
+                for (size_t v = 0; v < 8; ++v) {
+                    s0[v] += a0 * rhs[v];
+                    s1[v] += a1 * rhs[v];
+                    s2[v] += a2 * rhs[v];
+                    s3[v] += a3 * rhs[v];
+                }
+            }
+            for (size_t v = 0; v < 8; ++v) {
+                if constexpr (Subtract) {
+                    target[i * b + j + v] -= s0[v];
+                    target[(i + 1) * b + j + v] -= s1[v];
+                    target[(i + 2) * b + j + v] -= s2[v];
+                    target[(i + 3) * b + j + v] -= s3[v];
+                } else {
+                    target[i * b + j + v] += s0[v];
+                    target[(i + 1) * b + j + v] += s1[v];
+                    target[(i + 2) * b + j + v] += s2[v];
+                    target[(i + 3) * b + j + v] += s3[v];
+                }
+            }
+#endif
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct Panels {
+    const ProcessGrid& grid;
+    std::vector<double> left, right, transposed;
+    std::vector<size_t> slots;
+    std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+
+    explicit Panels(const ProcessGrid& g)
+        : grid(g), left(g.localRows * g.block * g.block),
+          right(g.localCols * g.block * g.block), transposed(right.size()),
+          slots(g.localCols), counts(g.rows), displacements(g.rows) {
+        // Group RHS tiles by their source process row for a column allgather.
+        std::vector<size_t> next(g.rows, 0);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j)
+            ++next[(g.col + j * g.cols) % g.rows];
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < g.rows; ++r) {
+            const size_t length = next[r];
+            next[r] = offset;
+            offset += length;
+        }
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j)
+            slots[j] = next[(g.col + j * g.cols) % g.rows]++;
+    }
+
+    void collect(const Matrix& matrix, size_t k, size_t first) {
+        const auto& g = grid;
+        const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+        const size_t begin = g.firstLocalRow(first);
+        const size_t length = (g.localRows - begin) * area;
+        if (g.col == static_cast<int>(k % g.cols)) {
+            for (size_t i = begin; i < g.localRows; ++i)
+                std::copy_n(matrix.local(i, k / g.cols), area, left.data() + i * area);
+        }
+        // Roots rotate with the block-cyclic panel ownership.
+        if (length)
+            MPI_Bcast(left.data() + begin * area, static_cast<int>(length),
+                      MPI_DOUBLE, static_cast<int>(k % g.cols), g.rowComm);
+
+        std::fill(counts.begin(), counts.end(), 0);
+        std::fill(displacements.begin(), displacements.end(), 0);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j) {
+            const size_t global = g.col + j * g.cols;
+            if (global < first) continue;
+            const int owner = static_cast<int>(global % g.rows);
+            if (!counts[owner])
+                displacements[owner] = static_cast<int>(slots[j] * area);
+            counts[owner] += static_cast<int>(area);
+            if (owner == g.row)
+                std::copy_n(left.data() + (global / g.rows) * area, area,
+                            right.data() + slots[j] * area);
+        }
+        double dummy = 0.0;
+        double* receive = right.empty() ? &dummy : right.data();
+        if (g.rows == g.cols) {
+            // On a square grid all RHS tiles in a process column share a root.
+            if (counts[g.col])
+                MPI_Bcast(receive + displacements[g.col], counts[g.col],
+                          MPI_DOUBLE, g.col, g.colComm);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DOUBLE, receive,
+                           counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, g.colComm);
+        }
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j) {
+            if (g.col + j * g.cols < first) continue;
+            const double* source = receive + slots[j] * area;
+            double* dest = transposed.data() + j * area;
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < g.block; ++i)
+                for (size_t t = 0; t < g.block; ++t)
+                    dest[t * g.block + i] = source[i * g.block + t];
+        }
+    }
+
+    template<bool Subtract>
+    void update(Matrix& matrix, size_t first) const {
+        const auto& g = grid;
+        const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+        for (size_t i = g.firstLocalRow(first); i < g.localRows; ++i) {
+            const size_t globalI = g.row + i * g.rows;
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j) {
+                const size_t globalJ = g.col + j * g.cols;
+                if (globalJ < first || globalJ > globalI) continue;
+                multiplyTile<Subtract>(matrix.local(i, j), left.data() + i * area,
+                                       transposed.data() + j * area, g.block);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+// Distributed symmetric product. Only the lower triangle is needed; it is
+// also sufficient to check every entry of the symmetric reconstruction.
+void symmetricProduct(const Matrix& input, Matrix& output, bool triangular) {
+    Panels panels(input.grid);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < input.grid.tiles; ++k) {
+        const size_t first = triangular ? k : 0;
+        panels.collect(input, k, first);
+        panels.update<false>(output, first);
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(Matrix& matrix, size_t n) {
+    const auto& g = matrix.grid;
+    Matrix random(g);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> rowSeeds(n);
+    // Record the original rand_r state at each row. This retains the exact
+    // seed-42 input while other ranks generate just their process-row data.
+    if (g.rank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            rowSeeds[i] = seed;
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+                if ((i / g.block) % g.rows == 0 && (j / g.block) % g.cols == 0)
+                    random.tile(i / g.block, j / g.block)[(i % g.block) * g.block + j % g.block] = value;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n;) {
+        const int length = static_cast<int>(std::min(n - offset,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())));
+        MPI_Bcast(rowSeeds.data() + offset, length, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += static_cast<size_t>(length);
+    }
+    if (g.rank != 0) {
+        for (size_t local = 0; local < g.localRows; ++local) {
+            const size_t tileRow = g.row + local * g.rows;
+            const size_t end = std::min(n, (tileRow + 1) * g.block);
+            for (size_t i = tileRow * g.block; i < end; ++i) {
+                unsigned int seed = rowSeeds[i];
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                    const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+                    if ((j / g.block) % g.cols == static_cast<size_t>(g.col))
+                        random.local(local, (j / g.block) / g.cols)[(i % g.block) * g.block + j % g.block] = value;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    symmetricProduct(random, matrix, false);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < g.tiles; ++k) {
+        if (k % g.rows != static_cast<size_t>(g.row) ||
+            k % g.cols != static_cast<size_t>(g.col)) continue;
+        double* diagonal = matrix.tile(k, k);
+        const size_t width = std::min(g.block, n - k * g.block);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i)
+            diagonal[i * g.block + i] += static_cast<double>(n);
+    }
+}

+bool choleskyDecomposition(Matrix& matrix, size_t n) {
+    const auto& g = matrix.grid;
+    const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+    Panels panels(g);
+    // The extra entry communicates a failing diagonal in the same broadcast.
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(area + 1), inverse(g.block);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < g.tiles; ++k) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(k % g.rows) * g.cols + static_cast<int>(k % g.cols);
+        const size_t width = std::min(g.block, n - k * g.block);
+        if (g.rank == owner) {
+            double* tile = matrix.tile(k, k);
+            size_t failed = 0;
+            diagonal[area] = factorDiagonal(tile, g.block, width, failed)
+                ? 0.0 : static_cast<double>(k * g.block + failed + 1);
+            std::copy_n(tile, area, diagonal.data());
+        }
+        MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(), static_cast<int>(area + 1), MPI_DOUBLE, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (diagonal[area] != 0.0) {
+            if (g.rank == 0)
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(diagonal[area] - 1));
+            return false;
+        }
+        if (k + 1 == g.tiles) break;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j)
+            inverse[j] = 1.0 / diagonal[j * g.block + j];
+        if (g.col == static_cast<int>(k % g.cols)) {
+            for (size_t i = g.firstLocalRow(k + 1); i < g.localRows; ++i) {
+                const size_t global = g.row + i * g.rows;
+                solvePanel(matrix.local(i, k / g.cols), diagonal.data(), inverse.data(),
+                           g.block, std::min(g.block, n - global * g.block), width);
             }

         }
-    }
-

-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        A[i * n + i] += n;
+        panels.collect(matrix, k, k + 1);
+        panels.update<true>(matrix, k + 1);
     }
+    return true;

+// Gathering is solely for the original -r interface, outside timed work.
+// Streaming one rank at a time limits root scratch storage to a local matrix,
+// and chunked messages avoid MPI's int count limit.
+std::vector<double> gatherFactor(const Matrix& matrix, size_t n) {
+    const auto& g = matrix.grid;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (g.rank != 0) {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < matrix.data.size();) {
+            const int length = static_cast<int>(std::min(matrix.data.size() - offset,
+                                          static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())));
+            MPI_Send(matrix.data.data() + offset, length, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 11, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            offset += static_cast<size_t>(length);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+    result.resize(n * n, 0.0);
+    std::vector<double> scratch;
+    const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+    for (int source = 0; source < g.size; ++source) {
+        const int row = source / g.cols, col = source % g.cols;
+        const size_t nr = ProcessGrid::count(g.tiles, row, g.rows);
+        const size_t nc = ProcessGrid::count(g.tiles, col, g.cols);
+        const double* packed = matrix.data.data();
+        if (source != 0) {
+            scratch.resize(nr * nc * area);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < scratch.size();) {
+                const int length = static_cast<int>(std::min(scratch.size() - offset,
+                                              static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())));
+                MPI_Recv(scratch.data() + offset, length, MPI_DOUBLE, source, 11,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += static_cast<size_t>(length);
+            }
+            packed = scratch.data();
+        }
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < nr; ++i) {
+            const size_t globalI = row + i * g.rows;
+            const size_t height = std::min(g.block, n - globalI * g.block);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < nc; ++j) {
+                const size_t globalJ = col + j * g.cols;
+                if (globalJ > globalI) continue;
+                const size_t width = std::min(g.block, n - globalJ * g.block);
+                const double* tile = packed + (i * nc + j) * area;
+                for (size_t r = 0; r < height; ++r)
+                    std::copy_n(tile + r * g.block, width,
+                                result.data() + (globalI * g.block + r) * n + globalJ * g.block);
+            }
+        }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    return result;
 }

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+
+// All matrices use square, row-major tiles on a 2D block-cyclic process grid.
+// A rank stores O(n^2 / ranks) matrix entries and O(n * block / sqrt(ranks))
+// panel entries on a balanced grid; complete matrices are never replicated.
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int rank = 0, size = 0;
+    int rows = 0, cols = 0, row = 0, col = 0;
+    size_t block = 64, tiles = 0, localRows = 0, localCols = 0;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL, colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+
+    explicit ProcessGrid(size_t n) {
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        rows = dims[0];
+        cols = dims[1];
+        row = rank / cols;
+        col = rank % cols;
+        // Smaller tiles expose enough work for small matrices and large grids.
+        while (block > 8 && n / block < 2 * static_cast<size_t>(std::max(rows, cols)))
+            block /= 2;
+        tiles = n / block + (n % block != 0);
+        localRows = count(tiles, row, rows);
+        localCols = count(tiles, col, cols);
+        // Allgatherv uses int counts/displacements for panel buffers.
+        const size_t maxPanelTiles = std::max(count(tiles, 0, rows),
+                                             count(tiles, 0, cols));
+        if (maxPanelTiles > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) / (block * block))
+            throw std::length_error("Matrix panels exceed MPI count limits");
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, col, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, col, row, &colComm);
+    }
+
+    static size_t count(size_t total, int coordinate, int stride) {
+        const size_t start = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+        return total > start ? 1 + (total - 1 - start) / static_cast<size_t>(stride) : 0;
+    }
+
+    size_t firstLocalRow(size_t first) const {
+        return first > static_cast<size_t>(row)
+            ? std::min(localRows, (first - row + rows - 1) / rows) : 0;
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        if (rowComm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        if (colComm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+    }
+};
+
+struct Matrix {
+    const ProcessGrid& grid;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    explicit Matrix(const ProcessGrid& g) : grid(g) {
+        const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+        if (g.localRows && g.localCols > data.max_size() / area / g.localRows)
+            throw std::length_error("Matrix is too large");
+        data.resize(g.localRows * g.localCols * area, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    double* local(size_t i, size_t j) {
+        return data.data() + (i * grid.localCols + j) * grid.block * grid.block;
+    }
+
+    const double* local(size_t i, size_t j) const {
+        return data.data() + (i * grid.localCols + j) * grid.block * grid.block;
+    }
+
+    double* tile(size_t i, size_t j) {
+        return local(i / grid.rows, j / grid.cols);
+    }
+};
+
+#if defined(__AVX2__)
+inline __m256d accumulateProduct(__m256d a, __m256d b, __m256d sum) {
+#if defined(__FMA__)
+    return _mm256_fmadd_pd(a, b, sum);
+#else
+    return _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_mul_pd(a, b), sum);
+#endif
+}
+
+template<bool Subtract>
+inline void storeSum(double* target, __m256d sum) {
+    const __m256d previous = _mm256_loadu_pd(target);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(target, Subtract ? _mm256_sub_pd(previous, sum)
+                                    : _mm256_add_pd(previous, sum));
+}
+#endif
+
+// Transposed RHS panels are reused for every local tile. The 4 x 8
+// register block avoids horizontal reductions and keeps independent SIMD
+// accumulators; other architectures use the same portable kernel below.
+template<bool Subtract>
+void multiplyTile(double* target, const double* left,
+                  const double* rightTransposed, size_t b) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < b; i += 4) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < b; j += 8) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__)
+            __m256d s00 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d s10 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d s20 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d s30 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < b; ++k) {
+                const double* rhs = rightTransposed + k * b + j;
+                const __m256d r0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(rhs);
+                const __m256d r1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(rhs + 4);
+                const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + i * b + k);
+                const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + (i + 1) * b + k);
+                const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + (i + 2) * b + k);
+                const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + (i + 3) * b + k);
+                s00 = accumulateProduct(a0, r0, s00);
+                s01 = accumulateProduct(a0, r1, s01);
+                s10 = accumulateProduct(a1, r0, s10);
+                s11 = accumulateProduct(a1, r1, s11);
+                s20 = accumulateProduct(a2, r0, s20);
+                s21 = accumulateProduct(a2, r1, s21);
+                s30 = accumulateProduct(a3, r0, s30);
+                s31 = accumulateProduct(a3, r1, s31);
+            }
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + i * b + j, s00);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + i * b + j + 4, s01);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 1) * b + j, s10);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 1) * b + j + 4, s11);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 2) * b + j, s20);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 2) * b + j + 4, s21);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 3) * b + j, s30);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 3) * b + j + 4, s31);
+#else
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < b; ++k) {
+                const double a0 = left[i * b + k];
+                const double a1 = left[(i + 1) * b + k];
+                const double a2 = left[(i + 2) * b + k];
+                const double a3 = left[(i + 3) * b + k];
+                const double* rhs = rightTransposed + k * b + j;
+                for (size_t v = 0; v < 8; ++v) {
+                    s0[v] += a0 * rhs[v];
+                    s1[v] += a1 * rhs[v];
+                    s2[v] += a2 * rhs[v];
+                    s3[v] += a3 * rhs[v];
+                }
+            }
+            for (size_t v = 0; v < 8; ++v) {
+                if constexpr (Subtract) {
+                    target[i * b + j + v] -= s0[v];
+                    target[(i + 1) * b + j + v] -= s1[v];
+                    target[(i + 2) * b + j + v] -= s2[v];
+                    target[(i + 3) * b + j + v] -= s3[v];
+                } else {
+                    target[i * b + j + v] += s0[v];
+                    target[(i + 1) * b + j + v] += s1[v];
+                    target[(i + 2) * b + j + v] += s2[v];
+                    target[(i + 3) * b + j + v] += s3[v];
+                }
+            }
+#endif
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct Panels {
+    const ProcessGrid& grid;
+    std::vector<double> left, right, transposed;
+    std::vector<size_t> slots;
+    std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+
+    explicit Panels(const ProcessGrid& g)
+        : grid(g), left(g.localRows * g.block * g.block),
+          right(g.localCols * g.block * g.block), transposed(right.size()),
+          slots(g.localCols), counts(g.rows), displacements(g.rows) {
+        // Group RHS tiles by their source process row for a column allgather.
+        std::vector<size_t> next(g.rows, 0);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j)
+            ++next[(g.col + j * g.cols) % g.rows];
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < g.rows; ++r) {
+            const size_t length = next[r];
+            next[r] = offset;
+            offset += length;
+        }
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j)
+            slots[j] = next[(g.col + j * g.cols) % g.rows]++;
+    }
+
+    void collect(const Matrix& matrix, size_t k, size_t first) {
+        const auto& g = grid;
+        const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+        const size_t begin = g.firstLocalRow(first);
+        const size_t length = (g.localRows - begin) * area;
+        if (g.col == static_cast<int>(k % g.cols)) {
+            for (size_t i = begin; i < g.localRows; ++i)
+                std::copy_n(matrix.local(i, k / g.cols), area, left.data() + i * area);
+        }
+        // Roots rotate with the block-cyclic panel ownership.
+        if (length)
+            MPI_Bcast(left.data() + begin * area, static_cast<int>(length),
+                      MPI_DOUBLE, static_cast<int>(k % g.cols), g.rowComm);
+
+        std::fill(counts.begin(), counts.end(), 0);
+        std::fill(displacements.begin(), displacements.end(), 0);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j) {
+            const size_t global = g.col + j * g.cols;
+            if (global < first) continue;
+            const int owner = static_cast<int>(global % g.rows);
+            if (!counts[owner])
+                displacements[owner] = static_cast<int>(slots[j] * area);
+            counts[owner] += static_cast<int>(area);
+            if (owner == g.row)
+                std::copy_n(left.data() + (global / g.rows) * area, area,
+                            right.data() + slots[j] * area);
+        }
+        double dummy = 0.0;
+        double* receive = right.empty() ? &dummy : right.data();
+        if (g.rows == g.cols) {
+            // On a square grid all RHS tiles in a process column share a root.
+            if (counts[g.col])
+                MPI_Bcast(receive + displacements[g.col], counts[g.col],
+                          MPI_DOUBLE, g.col, g.colComm);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DOUBLE, receive,
+                           counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, g.colComm);
+        }
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j) {
+            if (g.col + j * g.cols < first) continue;
+            const double* source = receive + slots[j] * area;
+            double* dest = transposed.data() + j * area;
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < g.block; ++i)
+                for (size_t t = 0; t < g.block; ++t)
+                    dest[t * g.block + i] = source[i * g.block + t];
+        }
+    }
+
+    template<bool Subtract>
+    void update(Matrix& matrix, size_t first) const {
+        const auto& g = grid;
+        const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+        for (size_t i = g.firstLocalRow(first); i < g.localRows; ++i) {
+            const size_t globalI = g.row + i * g.rows;
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j) {
+                const size_t globalJ = g.col + j * g.cols;
+                if (globalJ < first || globalJ > globalI) continue;
+                multiplyTile<Subtract>(matrix.local(i, j), left.data() + i * area,
+                                       transposed.data() + j * area, g.block);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+// Distributed symmetric product. Only the lower triangle is needed; it is
+// also sufficient to check every entry of the symmetric reconstruction.
+void symmetricProduct(const Matrix& input, Matrix& output, bool triangular) {
+    Panels panels(input.grid);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < input.grid.tiles; ++k) {
+        const size_t first = triangular ? k : 0;
+        panels.collect(input, k, first);
+        panels.update<false>(output, first);
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(Matrix& matrix, size_t n) {
+    const auto& g = matrix.grid;
+    Matrix random(g);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> rowSeeds(n);
+    // Record the original rand_r state at each row. This retains the exact
+    // seed-42 input while other ranks generate just their process-row data.
+    if (g.rank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            rowSeeds[i] = seed;
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+                if ((i / g.block) % g.rows == 0 && (j / g.block) % g.cols == 0)
+                    random.tile(i / g.block, j / g.block)[(i % g.block) * g.block + j % g.block] = value;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n;) {
+        const int length = static_cast<int>(std::min(n - offset,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())));
+        MPI_Bcast(rowSeeds.data() + offset, length, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += static_cast<size_t>(length);
+    }
+    if (g.rank != 0) {
+        for (size_t local = 0; local < g.localRows; ++local) {
+            const size_t tileRow = g.row + local * g.rows;
+            const size_t end = std::min(n, (tileRow + 1) * g.block);
+            for (size_t i = tileRow * g.block; i < end; ++i) {
+                unsigned int seed = rowSeeds[i];
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                    const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+                    if ((j / g.block) % g.cols == static_cast<size_t>(g.col))
+                        random.local(local, (j / g.block) / g.cols)[(i % g.block) * g.block + j % g.block] = value;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    symmetricProduct(random, matrix, false);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < g.tiles; ++k) {
+        if (k % g.rows != static_cast<size_t>(g.row) ||
+            k % g.cols != static_cast<size_t>(g.col)) continue;
+        double* diagonal = matrix.tile(k, k);
+        const size_t width = std::min(g.block, n - k * g.block);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i)
+            diagonal[i * g.block + i] += static_cast<double>(n);
+    }
+}

+// Gathering is solely for the original -r interface, outside timed work.
+// Streaming one rank at a time limits root scratch storage to a local matrix,

+std::vector<double> gatherFactor(const Matrix& matrix, size_t n) {
+    const auto& g = matrix.grid;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (g.rank != 0) {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < matrix.data.size();) {
+            const int length = static_cast<int>(std::min(matrix.data.size() - offset,

+            offset += static_cast<size_t>(length);

+        return result;

+        const int row = source / g.cols, col = source % g.cols;
+        const size_t nr = ProcessGrid::count(g.tiles, row, g.rows);
+        const size_t nc = ProcessGrid::count(g.tiles, col, g.cols);
+        const double* packed = matrix.data.data();
+        if (source != 0) {
+            scratch.resize(nr * nc * area);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < scratch.size();) {
+                const int length = static_cast<int>(std::min(scratch.size() - offset,
+                                              static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())));
+                MPI_Recv(scratch.data() + offset, length, MPI_DOUBLE, source, 11,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += static_cast<size_t>(length);
+            }
+            packed = scratch.data();
+        }
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < nr; ++i) {
+            const size_t globalI = row + i * g.rows;
+            const size_t height = std::min(g.block, n - globalI * g.block);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < nc; ++j) {
+                const size_t globalJ = col + j * g.cols;
+                if (globalJ > globalI) continue;
+                const size_t width = std::min(g.block, n - globalJ * g.block);
+                const double* tile = packed + (i * nc + j) * area;
+                for (size_t r = 0; r < height; ++r)
+                    std::copy_n(tile + r * g.block, width,

   330	
   331	bool factorDiagonal(double* tile, size_t b, size_t width, size_t& failed) {
   332	    for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
   333	        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
   334	            double sum = 0.0;
   335	            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k)
   336	                sum += tile[i * b + k] * tile[j * b + k];
   337	            const double value = tile[i * b + j] - sum;
   338	            if (i == j) {
   339	                if (!(value > 0.0) || !std::isfinite(value)) {
   340	                    failed = i;
   341	                    return false;
   342	                }
   343	                tile[i * b + j] = std::sqrt(value);
   344	            } else {
   345	                tile[i * b + j] = value / tile[j * b + j];
   346	            }
   347	        }
   348	        std::fill(tile + i * b + i + 1, tile + (i + 1) * b, 0.0);
   349	    }
   350	    return true;
   351	}
   352	
   353	void solvePanel(double* tile, const double* diagonal, const double* inverse,
   354	                size_t b, size_t height, size_t width) {
   355	    for (size_t i = 0; i < height; ++i) {
   356	        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
   357	            double sum = 0.0;
   358	            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k)
   359	                sum += tile[i * b + k] * diagonal[j * b + k];
   360	            tile[i * b + j] = (tile[i * b + j] - sum) * inverse[j];
   361	        }
   362	    }
   363	}
   364	
   365	bool choleskyDecomposition(Matrix& matrix, size_t n) {
   366	    const auto& g = matrix.grid;
   367	    const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
   368	    Panels panels(g);
   369	    // The extra entry communicates a failing diagonal in the same broadcast.
   370	    std::vector<double> diagonal(area + 1), inverse(g.block);
   371	    for (size_t k = 0; k < g.tiles; ++k) {
   372	        const int owner = static_cast<int>(k % g.rows) * g.cols + static_cast<int>(k % g.cols);
   373	        const size_t width = std::min(g.block, n - k * g.block);
   374	        if (g.rank == owner) {
   375	            double* tile = matrix.tile(k, k);
   376	            size_t failed = 0;
   377	            diagonal[area] = factorDiagonal(tile, g.block, width, failed)
   378	                ? 0.0 : static_cast<double>(k * g.block + failed + 1);
   379	            std::copy_n(tile, area, diagonal.data());

   382	        if (diagonal[area] != 0.0) {

   385	                       static_cast<size_t>(diagonal[area] - 1));
   386	            return false;

   388	        if (k + 1 == g.tiles) break;

   449	    if (g.rank != 0) {
   450	        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < matrix.data.size();) {
   451	            const int length = static_cast<int>(std::min(matrix.data.size() - offset,
   452	                                          static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())));
   453	            MPI_Send(matrix.data.data() + offset, length, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 11, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   454	            offset += static_cast<size_t>(length);
   455	        }
   456	        return result;
   457	    }
   458	    result.resize(n * n, 0.0);
   459	    std::vector<double> scratch;
   460	    const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
   461	    for (int source = 0; source < g.size; ++source) {
   462	        const int row = source / g.cols, col = source % g.cols;
   463	        const size_t nr = ProcessGrid::count(g.tiles, row, g.rows);
   464	        const size_t nc = ProcessGrid::count(g.tiles, col, g.cols);
   465	        const double* packed = matrix.data.data();
   466	        if (source != 0) {
   467	            scratch.resize(nr * nc * area);
   468	            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < scratch.size();) {
   469	                const int length = static_cast<int>(std::min(scratch.size() - offset,
   470	                                              static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())));
   471	                MPI_Recv(scratch.data() + offset, length, MPI_DOUBLE, source, 11,
   472	                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
   473	                offset += static_cast<size_t>(length);
   474	            }
   475	            packed = scratch.data();
   476	        }
   477	        for (size_t i = 0; i < nr; ++i) {
   478	            const size_t globalI = row + i * g.rows;
   479	            const size_t height = std::min(g.block, n - globalI * g.block);
   480	            for (size_t j = 0; j < nc; ++j) {
   481	                const size_t globalJ = col + j * g.cols;
   482	                if (globalJ > globalI) continue;
   483	                const size_t width = std::min(g.block, n - globalJ * g.block);
   484	                const double* tile = packed + (i * nc + j) * area;
   485	                for (size_t r = 0; r < height; ++r)
   486	                    std::copy_n(tile + r * g.block, width,
   487	                                result.data() + (globalI * g.block + r) * n + globalJ * g.block);
   488	            }
   489	        }
   490	    }
   491	    return result;
   492	}
   493	
   494	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   495	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   496	    printf("Options:\n");
   497	    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
   498	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   499	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   500	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   501	}
   502	
   503	int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, int rank) {
   504	    size_t n = 512;
   505	    bool validate = false, printResults = false;
   506	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   507	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   508	            const char* value = argv[++i];
   509	            char* end = nullptr;
   510	            errno = 0;
   511	            const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(value, &end, 10);
   512	            if (value[0] == '-' || value[0] == '\0' || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
   513	                parsed > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
   514	                if (rank == 0) printf("Invalid matrix size: %s\n", value);
   515	                return 1;
   516	            }
   517	            n = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
   518	            if (n && n > std::vector<double>().max_size() / n) {
   519	                if (rank == 0) printf("Matrix size is too large\n");
   520	                return 1;
   521	            }
   522	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   523	            validate = true;
   524	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   525	            printResults = true;
   526	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   527	            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
   528	            return 0;
   529	        } else {
   530	            if (rank == 0) {
   531	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   532	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   533	            }
   534	            return 1;
   535	        }
   536	    }
   537	
   538	    ProcessGrid grid(n);
   539	    if (rank == 0) {
   540	        printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
   541	        printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
   542	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   543	        printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
   544	    }
   545	    Matrix matrix(grid);
   546	    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(matrix, n);
   547	    std::optional<Matrix> original;
   548	    if (validate) {
   549	        original.emplace(grid);
   550	        original->data = matrix.data;
   551	    }
   552	    if (rank == 0) printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
   553	
   554	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   555	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   556	    const bool success = choleskyDecomposition(matrix, n);
   557	    double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   558	    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   559	    if (!success) {
   560	        if (rank == 0) printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
   561	        return 1;
   562	    }
   563	    if (rank == 0) {
   564	        printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", static_cast<long long>(elapsed * 1000.0));
   565	        const double ops = static_cast<double>(n) * n * n / 3.0;
   566	        printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", elapsed > 0.0 ? ops / elapsed / 1e9 : 0.0);
   567	    }
   568	    if (printResults) {
   569	        const std::vector<double> factor = gatherFactor(matrix, n);
   570	        if (rank == 0) print_results(factor, "CholeskyL");
   571	    }
   572	    if (validate) {
   573	        if (rank == 0) printf("Validating result...\n");
   574	        const bool valid = validateCholesky(matrix, *original, n);
   575	        if (rank == 0) printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   576	        return valid ? 0 : 1;
   577	    }
   578	    return 0;
   579	}
   580	
   581	} // namespace
   582	
   583	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   584	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   585	    int rank = 0;
   586	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   587	    int status = 1;
   588	    try {
   589	        status = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank);
   590	    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
   591	        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
   592	        // Do not leave other ranks blocked in a collective on allocation failure.
   593	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
   594	    }
   595	    MPI_Finalize();
   596	    return status;
   597	}

 #include <algorithm>
-#include <chrono>

 #include <cmath>
 #include <cstdio>

 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+
+// All matrices use square, row-major tiles on a 2D block-cyclic process grid.
+// A rank stores O(n^2 / ranks) matrix entries and O(n * block / sqrt(ranks))
+// panel entries on a balanced grid; complete matrices are never replicated.
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int rank = 0, size = 0;
+    int rows = 0, cols = 0, row = 0, col = 0;
+    size_t block = 64, tiles = 0, localRows = 0, localCols = 0;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL, colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+
+    explicit ProcessGrid(size_t n) {
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        rows = dims[0];
+        cols = dims[1];
+        row = rank / cols;
+        col = rank % cols;
+        // Smaller tiles expose enough work for small matrices and large grids.
+        while (block > 8 && n / block < 2 * static_cast<size_t>(std::max(rows, cols)))
+            block /= 2;
+        tiles = n / block + (n % block != 0);
+        localRows = count(tiles, row, rows);
+        localCols = count(tiles, col, cols);
+        // Allgatherv uses int counts/displacements for panel buffers.
+        const size_t maxPanelTiles = std::max(count(tiles, 0, rows),
+                                             count(tiles, 0, cols));
+        if (maxPanelTiles > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) / (block * block))
+            throw std::length_error("Matrix panels exceed MPI count limits");
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, col, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, col, row, &colComm);
+    }
+
+    static size_t count(size_t total, int coordinate, int stride) {
+        const size_t start = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+        return total > start ? 1 + (total - 1 - start) / static_cast<size_t>(stride) : 0;
+    }
+
+    size_t firstLocalRow(size_t first) const {
+        return first > static_cast<size_t>(row)
+            ? std::min(localRows, (first - row + rows - 1) / rows) : 0;
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        if (rowComm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        if (colComm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+    }
+};
+
+struct Matrix {
+    const ProcessGrid& grid;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    explicit Matrix(const ProcessGrid& g) : grid(g) {
+        const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+        if (g.localRows && g.localCols > data.max_size() / area / g.localRows)
+            throw std::length_error("Matrix is too large");
+        data.resize(g.localRows * g.localCols * area, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    double* local(size_t i, size_t j) {
+        return data.data() + (i * grid.localCols + j) * grid.block * grid.block;
+    }
+
+    const double* local(size_t i, size_t j) const {
+        return data.data() + (i * grid.localCols + j) * grid.block * grid.block;
+    }
+
+    double* tile(size_t i, size_t j) {
+        return local(i / grid.rows, j / grid.cols);
+    }
+};
+
+#if defined(__AVX2__)
+inline __m256d accumulateProduct(__m256d a, __m256d b, __m256d sum) {
+#if defined(__FMA__)
+    return _mm256_fmadd_pd(a, b, sum);
+#else
+    return _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_mul_pd(a, b), sum);
+#endif
+}
+
+template<bool Subtract>
+inline void storeSum(double* target, __m256d sum) {
+    const __m256d previous = _mm256_loadu_pd(target);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(target, Subtract ? _mm256_sub_pd(previous, sum)
+                                    : _mm256_add_pd(previous, sum));
+}
+#endif
+
+// Transposed RHS panels are reused for every local tile. The 4 x 8
+// register block avoids horizontal reductions and keeps independent SIMD
+// accumulators; other architectures use the same portable kernel below.
+template<bool Subtract>
+void multiplyTile(double* target, const double* left,
+                  const double* rightTransposed, size_t b) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < b; i += 4) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < b; j += 8) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__)
+            __m256d s00 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d s10 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d s20 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d s30 = _mm256_setzero_pd(), s31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < b; ++k) {
+                const double* rhs = rightTransposed + k * b + j;
+                const __m256d r0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(rhs);
+                const __m256d r1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(rhs + 4);
+                const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + i * b + k);
+                const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + (i + 1) * b + k);
+                const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + (i + 2) * b + k);
+                const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(left + (i + 3) * b + k);
+                s00 = accumulateProduct(a0, r0, s00);
+                s01 = accumulateProduct(a0, r1, s01);
+                s10 = accumulateProduct(a1, r0, s10);
+                s11 = accumulateProduct(a1, r1, s11);
+                s20 = accumulateProduct(a2, r0, s20);
+                s21 = accumulateProduct(a2, r1, s21);
+                s30 = accumulateProduct(a3, r0, s30);
+                s31 = accumulateProduct(a3, r1, s31);
+            }
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + i * b + j, s00);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + i * b + j + 4, s01);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 1) * b + j, s10);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 1) * b + j + 4, s11);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 2) * b + j, s20);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 2) * b + j + 4, s21);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 3) * b + j, s30);
+            storeSum<Subtract>(target + (i + 3) * b + j + 4, s31);
+#else
+            double s0[8] = {}, s1[8] = {}, s2[8] = {}, s3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < b; ++k) {
+                const double a0 = left[i * b + k];
+                const double a1 = left[(i + 1) * b + k];
+                const double a2 = left[(i + 2) * b + k];
+                const double a3 = left[(i + 3) * b + k];
+                const double* rhs = rightTransposed + k * b + j;
+                for (size_t v = 0; v < 8; ++v) {
+                    s0[v] += a0 * rhs[v];
+                    s1[v] += a1 * rhs[v];
+                    s2[v] += a2 * rhs[v];
+                    s3[v] += a3 * rhs[v];
+                }
+            }
+            for (size_t v = 0; v < 8; ++v) {
+                if constexpr (Subtract) {
+                    target[i * b + j + v] -= s0[v];
+                    target[(i + 1) * b + j + v] -= s1[v];
+                    target[(i + 2) * b + j + v] -= s2[v];
+                    target[(i + 3) * b + j + v] -= s3[v];
+                } else {
+                    target[i * b + j + v] += s0[v];
+                    target[(i + 1) * b + j + v] += s1[v];
+                    target[(i + 2) * b + j + v] += s2[v];
+                    target[(i + 3) * b + j + v] += s3[v];
+                }
+            }
+#endif
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct Panels {
+    const ProcessGrid& grid;
+    std::vector<double> left, right, transposed;
+    std::vector<size_t> slots;
+    std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+
+    explicit Panels(const ProcessGrid& g)
+        : grid(g), left(g.localRows * g.block * g.block),
+          right(g.localCols * g.block * g.block), transposed(right.size()),
+          slots(g.localCols), counts(g.rows), displacements(g.rows) {
+        // Group RHS tiles by their source process row for a column allgather.
+        std::vector<size_t> next(g.rows, 0);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j)
+            ++next[(g.col + j * g.cols) % g.rows];
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < g.rows; ++r) {
+            const size_t length = next[r];
+            next[r] = offset;
+            offset += length;
+        }
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j)
+            slots[j] = next[(g.col + j * g.cols) % g.rows]++;
+    }
+
+    void collect(const Matrix& matrix, size_t k, size_t first) {
+        const auto& g = grid;
+        const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+        const size_t begin = g.firstLocalRow(first);
+        const size_t length = (g.localRows - begin) * area;
+        if (g.col == static_cast<int>(k % g.cols)) {
+            for (size_t i = begin; i < g.localRows; ++i)
+                std::copy_n(matrix.local(i, k / g.cols), area, left.data() + i * area);
+        }
+        // Roots rotate with the block-cyclic panel ownership.
+        if (length)
+            MPI_Bcast(left.data() + begin * area, static_cast<int>(length),
+                      MPI_DOUBLE, static_cast<int>(k % g.cols), g.rowComm);
+
+        std::fill(counts.begin(), counts.end(), 0);
+        std::fill(displacements.begin(), displacements.end(), 0);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j) {
+            const size_t global = g.col + j * g.cols;
+            if (global < first) continue;
+            const int owner = static_cast<int>(global % g.rows);
+            if (!counts[owner])
+                displacements[owner] = static_cast<int>(slots[j] * area);
+            counts[owner] += static_cast<int>(area);
+            if (owner == g.row)
+                std::copy_n(left.data() + (global / g.rows) * area, area,
+                            right.data() + slots[j] * area);
+        }
+        double dummy = 0.0;
+        double* receive = right.empty() ? &dummy : right.data();
+        if (g.rows == g.cols) {
+            // On a square grid all RHS tiles in a process column share a root.
+            if (counts[g.col])
+                MPI_Bcast(receive + displacements[g.col], counts[g.col],
+                          MPI_DOUBLE, g.col, g.colComm);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DOUBLE, receive,
+                           counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, g.colComm);
+        }
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j) {
+            if (g.col + j * g.cols < first) continue;
+            const double* source = receive + slots[j] * area;
+            double* dest = transposed.data() + j * area;
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < g.block; ++i)
+                for (size_t t = 0; t < g.block; ++t)
+                    dest[t * g.block + i] = source[i * g.block + t];
+        }
+    }
+
+    template<bool Subtract>
+    void update(Matrix& matrix, size_t first) const {
+        const auto& g = grid;
+        const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+        for (size_t i = g.firstLocalRow(first); i < g.localRows; ++i) {
+            const size_t globalI = g.row + i * g.rows;
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < g.localCols; ++j) {
+                const size_t globalJ = g.col + j * g.cols;
+                if (globalJ < first || globalJ > globalI) continue;
+                multiplyTile<Subtract>(matrix.local(i, j), left.data() + i * area,
+                                       transposed.data() + j * area, g.block);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+// Distributed symmetric product. Only the lower triangle is needed; it is
+// also sufficient to check every entry of the symmetric reconstruction.
+void symmetricProduct(const Matrix& input, Matrix& output, bool triangular) {
+    Panels panels(input.grid);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < input.grid.tiles; ++k) {
+        const size_t first = triangular ? k : 0;
+        panels.collect(input, k, first);
+        panels.update<false>(output, first);
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(Matrix& matrix, size_t n) {
+    const auto& g = matrix.grid;
+    Matrix random(g);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> rowSeeds(n);
+    // Record the original rand_r state at each row. This retains the exact
+    // seed-42 input while other ranks generate just their process-row data.
+    if (g.rank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            rowSeeds[i] = seed;
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+                if ((i / g.block) % g.rows == 0 && (j / g.block) % g.cols == 0)
+                    random.tile(i / g.block, j / g.block)[(i % g.block) * g.block + j % g.block] = value;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n;) {
+        const int length = static_cast<int>(std::min(n - offset,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())));
+        MPI_Bcast(rowSeeds.data() + offset, length, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        offset += static_cast<size_t>(length);
+    }
+    if (g.rank != 0) {
+        for (size_t local = 0; local < g.localRows; ++local) {
+            const size_t tileRow = g.row + local * g.rows;
+            const size_t end = std::min(n, (tileRow + 1) * g.block);
+            for (size_t i = tileRow * g.block; i < end; ++i) {
+                unsigned int seed = rowSeeds[i];
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                    const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 0.5;
+                    if ((j / g.block) % g.cols == static_cast<size_t>(g.col))
+                        random.local(local, (j / g.block) / g.cols)[(i % g.block) * g.block + j % g.block] = value;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    symmetricProduct(random, matrix, false);

+bool choleskyDecomposition(Matrix& matrix, size_t n) {
+    const auto& g = matrix.grid;
+    const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+    Panels panels(g);
+    // The extra entry communicates a failing diagonal in the same broadcast.
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(area + 1), inverse(g.block);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < g.tiles; ++k) {
+        const int owner = static_cast<int>(k % g.rows) * g.cols + static_cast<int>(k % g.cols);
+        const size_t width = std::min(g.block, n - k * g.block);
+        if (g.rank == owner) {
+            double* tile = matrix.tile(k, k);
+            size_t failed = 0;
+            diagonal[area] = factorDiagonal(tile, g.block, width, failed)
+                ? 0.0 : static_cast<double>(k * g.block + failed + 1);
+            std::copy_n(tile, area, diagonal.data());
+        }
+        MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(), static_cast<int>(area + 1), MPI_DOUBLE, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (diagonal[area] != 0.0) {
+            if (g.rank == 0)
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(diagonal[area] - 1));
+            return false;
+        }
+        if (k + 1 == g.tiles) break;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j)
+            inverse[j] = 1.0 / diagonal[j * g.block + j];
+        if (g.col == static_cast<int>(k % g.cols)) {
+            for (size_t i = g.firstLocalRow(k + 1); i < g.localRows; ++i) {
+                const size_t global = g.row + i * g.rows;
+                solvePanel(matrix.local(i, k / g.cols), diagonal.data(), inverse.data(),
+                           g.block, std::min(g.block, n - global * g.block), width);

+        panels.update<true>(matrix, k + 1);
     }

+// Gathering is solely for the original -r interface, outside timed work.
+// Streaming one rank at a time limits root scratch storage to a local matrix,
+// and chunked messages avoid MPI's int count limit.
+std::vector<double> gatherFactor(const Matrix& matrix, size_t n) {
+    const auto& g = matrix.grid;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (g.rank != 0) {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < matrix.data.size();) {
+            const int length = static_cast<int>(std::min(matrix.data.size() - offset,
+                                          static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())));
+            MPI_Send(matrix.data.data() + offset, length, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 11, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            offset += static_cast<size_t>(length);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+    result.resize(n * n, 0.0);
+    std::vector<double> scratch;
+    const size_t area = g.block * g.block;
+    for (int source = 0; source < g.size; ++source) {
+        const int row = source / g.cols, col = source % g.cols;
+        const size_t nr = ProcessGrid::count(g.tiles, row, g.rows);
+        const size_t nc = ProcessGrid::count(g.tiles, col, g.cols);
+        const double* packed = matrix.data.data();
+        if (source != 0) {
+            scratch.resize(nr * nc * area);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < scratch.size();) {
+                const int length = static_cast<int>(std::min(scratch.size() - offset,
+                                              static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())));
+                MPI_Recv(scratch.data() + offset, length, MPI_DOUBLE, source, 11,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += static_cast<size_t>(length);
+            }
+            packed = scratch.data();
+        }
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < nr; ++i) {
+            const size_t globalI = row + i * g.rows;
+            const size_t height = std::min(g.block, n - globalI * g.block);
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < nc; ++j) {
+                const size_t globalJ = col + j * g.cols;
+                if (globalJ > globalI) continue;
+                const size_t width = std::min(g.block, n - globalJ * g.block);
+                const double* tile = packed + (i * nc + j) * area;
+                for (size_t r = 0; r < height; ++r)
+                    std::copy_n(tile + r * g.block, width,
+                                result.data() + (globalI * g.block + r) * n + globalJ * g.block);
+            }
+        }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    return result;
 }
